A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE
Audit order: 181

Clause / section / page: 8.3.3.3.3.2 / Part I-A Chapter 8 - Survival / 25
Source URL: https://wwwcdn.imo.org/localresources/en/KnowledgeCentre/IndexofIMOResolutions/MSCResolutions/MSC.385%2894%29.pdf
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/MSC.385(94).pdf

Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-IMO-075-20260915T111310881670Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
When required, personal and group survival equipment sufficient for 110% of the persons on board shall be stowed in easily accessible locations, as close as practical to the muster or embarkation stations.
Normalized requirement:
IF personal_or_group_survival_equipment_required = true, THEN available_survival_equipment_capacity >= ceil(persons_on_board * 1.10) AND storage_accessibility_status = accessible.
Required inputs: Persons-on-board count, equipment capacity, and storage-location evidence

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO_075_Shape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:personalOrGroupSurvivalEquipmentRequired ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The survival-equipment applicability status must be provided."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:personalOrGroupSurvivalEquipmentRequired ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:personsOnBoard ;
                sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "Persons on board must be provided when personal or group survival equipment is required."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:availableSurvivalEquipmentCapacity ;
                sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "Available survival equipment capacity must be provided when personal or group survival equipment is required."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:storageAccessibilityStatus ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:message "Required survival equipment must be recorded as stored in an accessible location."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:storageDistanceToMusterOrEmbarkation ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                        sh:minCount 1
                    ]
                ] ;
                sh:message "Required survival equipment storage distance to the muster or embarkation stations must be provided as a length in metres."
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "Available survival equipment capacity must be at least 110 percent of the persons on board." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:personsOnBoard ?persons ;
                              nltl:availableSurvivalEquipmentCapacity ?capacity .
                        FILTER (
                            datatype(?persons) = xsd:integer &&
                            datatype(?capacity) = xsd:integer &&
                            (?capacity * 10) < (?persons * 11)
                        )
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:personalOrGroupSurvivalEquipmentRequired ;
                sh:hasValue false
            ]
        ]
    ) .

```

```turtle
                sh:path nltl:availableSurvivalEquipmentCapacity ;
                sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;

            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:storageAccessibilityStatus ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:message "Required survival equipment must be stored in an accessible location."
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
```

====================================================================================================
REQUIREMENT 182: TRF-016
====================================================================================================

A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE
Audit order: 182
Batch: BATCH_19
Requirement ID: TRF-016
Source family: TRAFICOM
Verification mode: DIRECT_CALCULATION
Source document: Ice Class Regulations and the Application Thereof
Source revision: TRAFICOM/68863/03.04.01.00/2021
Clause / section / page: 2.2 / Maximum and minimum draught fore and aft / 7
Source URL: https://www.finlex.fi/en/authorities/regulations/traficom-transport-maritime/2021/47238
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/TRAFICOM.pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 8ab845347770c253433b8bad9b1fbe5d6ac71b3d07a1a28dae8a32cfde732ddc
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-016-20260915T122611566235Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The ship shall always be loaded down at least to the draught of LIWL amidships when navigating in ice. Any ballast tank, situated above the LIWL and needed to load down the ship to this water line, shall be equipped with devices to prevent the water from freezing. In determining the LIWL, regard shall be paid to the need to ensure a reasonable degree of ice-going capability in ballast. The highest point of the propeller shall be submerged and if possible at a depth of at least h_i below the water surface in all loading conditions. The forward draught shall be at least (2 + 0.00025*Delta)*h_i [m], but need not exceed 4*h_i (2.1), where Delta is the displacement of the ship [t] determined from the waterline on the UIWL (see section 2.1). Where multiple waterlines are used for determining the UIWL, the displacement must be determined from the waterline corresponding to the greatest displacement. h_i is the level ice thickness [m] according to section 4.2.1.
Normalized requirement:
When navigating in ice, draught_amidships_m >= LIWL_draught_amidships_m. Any ballast tank above LIWL that is needed to reach LIWL shall have freezing_prevention_present = true. propeller_highest_point_submerged = true. forward_draught_m >= min((2 + 0.00025*displacement_UIWL_t)*level_ice_thickness_m, 4*level_ice_thickness_m). If multiple UIWL waterlines exist, use the displacement at the greatest-displacement UIWL.
Required inputs: UIWL, LIWL

Locked context, definitions, ownership, units, and dependency contract:
{
  "dependency_contract": {

```turtle
                sh:minCount 1 ;

                sh:node [
                    sh:property [

                    ] ;
                    sh:property [

            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasBallastTank ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:situatedAboveLowerIceWaterline ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:usedToReachLowerIceWaterline ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:freezingPreventionPresent ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "Draught amidships must be at least the LIWL draught amidships when navigating in ice." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:navigatingInIce true ;
                              nltl:draughtAmidships ?draughtNode ;
                              nltl:liwlDraughtAmidships ?liwlNode .
                        ?draughtNode qudt:numericValue ?draught .
                        ?liwlNode qudt:numericValue ?liwl .
                        FILTER (?draught < ?liwl)
                    }
                """
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "Each ballast tank situated above the LIWL and needed to reach it must have freezing prevention." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:navigatingInIce true ;
                              nltl:hasBallastTank ?tank .
                        ?tank nltl:situatedAboveLowerIceWaterline true ;
                              nltl:usedToReachLowerIceWaterline true .
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            ?tank nltl:freezingPreventionPresent true
                        }
                    }
                """
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "Forward draught must meet the applicable level-ice forward-draught threshold using the greatest-displacement UIWL." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:navigatingInIce true ;
                              nltl:hasUpperIceWaterline ?uiwl ;
                              nltl:levelIceThickness ?hiNode ;
                              nltl:forwardDraught ?forwardNode .
                        ?uiwl nltl:waterlineDisplacement ?displacementNode .
                        ?displacementNode qudt:numericValue ?delta .
                        ?hiNode qudt:numericValue ?hi .
                        ?forwardNode qudt:numericValue ?forward .
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            $this nltl:hasUpperIceWaterline ?otherUiwl .
                            ?otherUiwl nltl:waterlineDisplacement ?otherDisplacementNode .
                            ?otherDisplacementNode qudt:numericValue ?otherDelta .
                            FILTER (?otherDelta > ?delta)
                        }
                        FILTER (
                            ?forward <
                            IF(
                                ((2 + (0.00025 * ?delta)) * ?hi) < (4 * ?hi),
                                ((2 + (0.00025 * ?delta)) * ?hi),
                                (4 * ?hi)
                            )
                        )
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
    ) .
```

CASE TRF-050-P02
Original expected verdict: PASS
Original rationale: Scalloping is present only at a shell-plate butt crossing, the explicit exception.
Source clause: 4.4.4.2
Test pattern: UNSPECIFIED
RDF path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/evaluation/BEHAVIORAL_RDF_R13/rdf/TRF/TRF-050/TRF-050-P02.ttl

Full unmodified RDF Turtle:
```turtle
@prefix ex: <https://w3id.org/nltl/benchmark/traficom-batch-e/TRF-050-P02/> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

ex:frame a nltl:frame ;
    nltl:frameAttachment ex:attachmentRecord .
```

```turtle
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:frameAttachmentRecord ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:frameShellWeldType ;
```
```turtle
        sh:class nltl:weldTypeValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
```

```turtle
        sh:message "Each frame attachment record shall specify a double continuous weld."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shellPlateButtCrossing ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each frame attachment record shall state whether a shell plate butt is crossed."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:shellPlateButtCrossing ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ]
        ]

                sh:path nltl:shellPlateButtCrossing ;
                sh:hasValue false
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:scallopingPresent ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue false ;
                sh:message "Scalloping is not allowed when the frame attachment does not cross a shell plate butt."
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Each frame attachment record shall be linked from a frame by frameAttachment." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    ?frame nltl:frameAttachment $this .
                }
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Discharge of sewage into the sea from category A and B ships constructed on or after 1 January 2017 and all passenger ships constructed on or after 1 January 2017 is prohibited, except when such discharges are in compliance with paragraph 4.2.1.3 of this chapter.

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<nltl:generated-shape-IMO-118> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A construction date is required to determine applicability of the sewage-discharge prohibition."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:polarShipCategoryA
            nltl:polarShipCategoryB
            nltl:polarShipCategoryC
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A valid polar ship category is required to determine applicability of the sewage-discharge prohibition."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:sewageDischargeToSea ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarCodeSewageParagraph4Point2Point1Point3ComplianceStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "For a ship constructed on or after 1 January 2017 that is Category A, Category B, or a passenger ship, sewage discharge to sea must be false unless compliance with paragraph 4.2.1.3 is recorded as true." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:constructionDate ?constructionDate .
                FILTER (
                    datatype(?constructionDate) = xsd:date &&
                    ?constructionDate >= "2017-01-01"^^xsd:date
                )
                {
                    $this nltl:shipCategory ?category .
                    FILTER (
                        ?category = nltl:polarShipCategoryA ||
                        ?category = nltl:polarShipCategoryB
                    )
                }
                UNION
                {
                    $this rdf:type nltl:passengerShip .
                }
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:polarCodeSewageParagraph4Point2Point1Point3ComplianceStatus ?compliance .
                    FILTER (?compliance = true)
                }
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:sewageDischargeToSea ?discharge .
                    FILTER (?discharge = false)
                }
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-062-ship>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerBladeTipHullClearance ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "A propeller blade tip to hull clearance, including the stern-frame context, must be provided as a metre quantity."
    ] .
```

REQUIREMENT 189: TRF-014
====================================================================================================

A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE
Audit order: 189
Batch: BATCH_19
Requirement ID: TRF-014
Source family: TRAFICOM
Verification mode: DIRECT_STATIC
Source document: Ice Class Regulations and the Application Thereof
Source revision: TRAFICOM/68863/03.04.01.00/2021
Clause / section / page: 2.2 / Maximum and minimum draught fore and aft / 7
Source URL: https://www.finlex.fi/en/authorities/regulations/traficom-transport-maritime/2021/47238
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/TRAFICOM.pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 8ab845347770c253433b8bad9b1fbe5d6ac71b3d07a1a28dae8a32cfde732ddc
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-014-20260915T122241413887Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
Restrictions on draughts when operating in ice shall be documented and kept on board readily available to the master. The maximum and minimum ice class draughts fore, amidships and aft shall be indicated in the class certificate. For ships built on or after 1 July 2007, if the summer load line in fresh water is anywhere located at a higher level than the UIWL, the ship's sides are to be provided with a warning triangle and with an ice class draught mark at the maximum permissible ice class draught amidships (see Annex III). Ships built before 1 July 2007 shall be provided with such a marking, if the UIWL is below the summer load line, not later than the first scheduled dry docking after 1 July 2007.
Normalized requirement:
Restrictions on draughts when operating in ice shall be documented and kept on board readily available to the master. The maximum and minimum ice class draughts fore, amidships and aft shall be indicated in the class certificate. For ships built on or after 1 July 2007, if the summer load line in fresh water is anywhere located at a higher level than the UIWL, the ship's sides are to be provided with a warning triangle and with an ice class draught mark at the maximum permissible ice class draught amidships (see Annex III). Ships built before 1 July 2007 shall be provided with such a marking, if the UIWL is below the summer load line, not later than the first scheduled dry docking after 1 July 2007.

```turtle
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            SELECT ?this
            WHERE {
                ?this nltl:summerLoadLineFreshWaterDraught ?summerDraught ;
                      nltl:upperIceWaterlineDraught ?uiwlDraught .

                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
```
```turtle
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ]

        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The applicable ice-class marking condition, including the assessment-date and dry-docking-date rule, is not satisfied." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:constructionDate ?constructionDate ;
                      nltl:assessmentDate ?assessmentDate ;
                      nltl:summerLoadLineFreshWaterDraught/qudt:numericValue ?summerDraught ;
                      nltl:upperIceWaterlineDraught/qudt:numericValue ?uiwlDraught ;
                      nltl:hasClassCertificate/nltl:maximumIceClassDraughtAmidships/qudt:numericValue ?maximumAmidships .

                OPTIONAL { $this nltl:warningTrianglePresent ?warningTriangle . }
                OPTIONAL { $this nltl:iceClassDraughtMarkPresent ?markPresent . }
                OPTIONAL { $this nltl:iceClassDraughtMarkDraughtAmidships/qudt:numericValue ?markDraught . }
                OPTIONAL { $this nltl:firstScheduledDryDockingDate ?dryDockingDate . }

                BIND(
                    (
                        (
                            ?constructionDate >= "2007-07-01"^^xsd:date &&
                            ?summerDraught > ?uiwlDraught &&
                            ?warningTriangle = true &&
                            ?markPresent = true &&
                            ?markDraught = ?maximumAmidships
                        )
                        ||
                        (
                            ?constructionDate < "2007-07-01"^^xsd:date &&
                            ?warningTriangle = true &&
                            ?markPresent = true &&
                            ?markDraught = ?maximumAmidships
                        )
                        ||
                        (
                            ?constructionDate < "2007-07-01"^^xsd:date &&
                            (
                                ?warningTriangle != true ||
                                ?markPresent != true ||
                                ?markDraught != ?maximumAmidships
                            ) &&
                            BOUND(?dryDockingDate) &&
                            (
                                ?dryDockingDate > ?assessmentDate ||
                                (
                                    ?dryDockingDate <= ?assessmentDate &&
                                    ?warningTriangle = true &&
                                    ?markPresent = true &&
                                    ?markDraught = ?maximumAmidships
                                )
                            )
                        )
                    )
                    AS ?validBranch
                )
                FILTER (?validBranch = false)
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .

gen:TRF014ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:assessmentDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;

        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship must provide exactly one assessment date."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship must provide exactly one construction date."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:iceClassValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship must provide one Finnish-Swedish ice-class value."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:summerLoadLineFreshWaterDraught ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The summer load line fresh-water draught must be provided in metres."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:upperIceWaterlineDraught ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The upper ice waterline draught must be provided in metres."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:retainedOnBoard ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The ice draught restriction information must be retained on board."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:readilyAvailableToMaster ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The ice draught restriction information must be readily available to the master."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasClassCertificate ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class nltl:classCertificate ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:maximumIceClassDraughtFore ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node gen:MetreQuantityShape
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:maximumIceClassDraughtAmidships ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node gen:MetreQuantityShape
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:maximumIceClassDraughtAft ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node gen:MetreQuantityShape
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:minimumIceClassDraughtFore ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node gen:MetreQuantityShape
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:minimumIceClassDraughtAmidships ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node gen:MetreQuantityShape
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:minimumIceClassDraughtAft ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node gen:MetreQuantityShape
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The class certificate must indicate all maximum and minimum ice-class draughts fore, amidships, and aft."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasIceDraughtRestrictionDocument ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class nltl:iceDraughtRestrictionDocument
        ] ;
        sh:message "The ice draught restrictions must be documented."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:and (
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
                        sh:minInclusive "2007-07-01"^^xsd:date
                    ]
                ]
                gen:SummerNotHigherShape
            )
        ]
        [
            sh:and (
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
                        sh:minInclusive "2007-07-01"^^xsd:date
                    ]
                ]
                gen:SummerHigherShape
                gen:MarkingShape
            )
        ]
        [
            sh:and (
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
                        sh:maxExclusive "2007-07-01"^^xsd:date
                    ]
                ]
                gen:SummerNotHigherShape
            )
        ]
        [
            sh:and (
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
                        sh:maxExclusive "2007-07-01"^^xsd:date
                    ]
                ]
                gen:SummerHigherShape
                [
                    sh:or (
                        gen:MarkingShape
                        [
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path nltl:firstScheduledDryDockingDate ;
                                sh:datatype xsd:date ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                                sh:lessThan nltl:assessmentDate
                            ]
                        ]
                        [
                            sh:and (
                                [
                                    sh:property [
                                        sh:path nltl:firstScheduledDryDockingDate ;
                                        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
                                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                                        sh:lessThanOrEquals nltl:assessmentDate
                                    ]
                                ]
                                gen:MarkingShape
                            )
                        ]
                    )
                ]
            )
        )
    ) ;
    sh:message "The applicable ice-class marking condition must be satisfied for the ship's construction date, draught relationship, and scheduled dry-docking date." .

gen:MetreQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue unit:M ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

gen:MarkingShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:warningTrianglePresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClassDraughtMarkPresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClassDraughtMarkDraughtAmidships ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:MetreQuantityShape
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The ice-class draught mark amidships must equal the maximum permissible ice-class draught amidships in the class certificate." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceClassDraughtMarkDraughtAmidships/qudt:numericValue ?markValue .
                $this nltl:hasClassCertificate ?certificate .
                ?certificate nltl:maximumIceClassDraughtAmidships/qudt:numericValue ?certificateValue .
                FILTER (?markValue != ?certificateValue)
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:SummerHigherShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The summer load line fresh-water draught must be higher than the upper ice waterline draught for this applicability branch." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:summerLoadLineFreshWaterDraught/qudt:numericValue ?summerValue .
                $this nltl:upperIceWaterlineDraught/qudt:numericValue ?uiwlValue .
                FILTER (?summerValue <= ?uiwlValue)
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:SummerNotHigherShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The non-applicable draught branch requires the summer load line fresh-water draught not to exceed the upper ice waterline draught." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:summerLoadLineFreshWaterDraught/qudt:numericValue ?summerValue .
                $this nltl:upperIceWaterlineDraught/qudt:numericValue ?uiwlValue .
                FILTER (?summerValue > ?uiwlValue)
            }
        """
    ] .

```

Original source text:
When calculating the section modulus and shear area of a framing member, net thicknesses of the web, flange (if fitted) and attached shell plating are to be used. The shear area of a framing member may include that material contained over the full depth of the member, i.e. web area including portion of flange, if fitted, but excluding attached shell plating.
Normalized requirement:
When calculating the section modulus and shear area of a framing member, net thicknesses of the web, flange (if fitted) and attached shell plating are to be used. The shear area of a framing member may include that material contained over the full depth of the member, i.e. web area including portion of flange, if fitted, but excluding attached shell plating.
Required inputs: sectionModulusZ, shearAreaA, thickness

```turtle
    ] ;
    sh:or (
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:I2_029_ShipShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasCalculationCase ;
        sh:class nltl:calculationCase ;
        sh:node gen:I2_029_CalculationCaseShape
    ] .

gen:I2_029_CalculationCaseShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:calculationCase ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:attachedShellPlatingIncludedInSectionModulus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The calculation case must include attached shell plating in the section modulus."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:attachedShellPlatingExcludedFromShearArea ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The calculation case must exclude attached shell plating from the shear area."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:flangeMaterialIncludedInShearArea ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The calculation case must explicitly represent whether flange material is included in the shear area."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:sectionCalculationCaseStructuralMember ;
        sh:class nltl:structuralMember ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:I2_029_StructuralMemberShape ;
        sh:message "The calculation case must link to its evaluated structural member."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "When flange material is included in the shear area, every linked structural member must record that a flange is fitted." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:flangeMaterialIncludedInShearArea true .
                $this nltl:sectionCalculationCaseStructuralMember ?member .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    ?member nltl:flangeFitted true .
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:I2_029_StructuralMemberShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:structuralMember .

```

F. MANUAL REVIEW CHECKLIST — DO NOT PREFILL GRADES
Q1 Applicability: Does the shape activate for the correct ships, conditions, and scope?
Q2 Ownership / paths: Are target ownership and property paths correct?
Q3 Required constraints: Are all mandatory obligations represented?
Q4 Units / calculation: Are quantities, units, formulae, and comparisons correct?
Q5 Branches / boundaries: Are alternatives, exceptions, equality boundaries, and conditionals correct?
Q6 No extra restrictions: Does the shape avoid requirements not supported by the source?
Q7 Traceability / clarity: Is the implementation understandable and traceable to the cited evidence?
Record grades, reviewer identity, evidence, and notes in the workbook. Do not infer full-requirement correctness from these two cases.
